Actual Answer

Extension hose options for the AIS Clear / Arctic Ice Clear cold therapy unit from PMT are:

1) Extension Hose for AIS Clear Cold Therapy Unit (Universal Pad)
• Product name: Extension hose for AIS Clear Cold Therapy Unit  
• Product code: CTU2CHOSE  
• Compatible with: Coolman, Leonns, Arctic Ice Clear, Oasis Space  
• Size: 5' length  
• Couplings: Leak-proof couplings included  
• Description: Extension hose for AIS Clear Cold Therapy Unit  
• Specifications: Length (cm): 1 | Width (cm): 1 | Height (cm): 1  
• Model: Extension Hose

2) Extension Hose for AIS Clear Cold Therapy Unit for Back Pad
• Product name: Extension hose for AIS Clear Cold Therapy Unit for back pad  
• Product code: CTU2CHOSEEXT  
• Compatible with: Coolman, Leonns, Arctic Ice Clear, Oasis Space  
• Size: 5' length  
• Couplings: Leak-proof couplings included  
• Description: Extension hose for AIS Clear Cold Therapy Unit  
• Specifications: Product weight (lbs): 1  
• Model: Extension Hose

Both hoses are designed specifically for the AIS/Arctic Ice Clear style cold therapy machines and include leak-proof couplings, with a 5-foot length to extend the distance between the cooler and the therapy pad. For purchasing or more details, you can browse PMT’s water therapy accessories on https://paintechnology.com or contact customer service for help selecting the correct hose for your specific pad type (universal vs. back pad).

Actual Answer

A 1-year warranty is given to all devices, excluding braces, Mobi Cushions, powered seat lift devices, powered chairs, Thermacycle, and hot/cold therapy units, which carry a 6‑month warranty on the unit and pad. This warranty is for replacement of like items only. If Pain Management Technologies (PMT) no longer stocks them, then PMT will upgrade you to a like model. Outdated technology is not covered by warranty if the item is no longer available on the common market as a new product.

Warranty policy does not include accessories supplied with the device. For example, lead wires, battery, and carrying case are not covered. Shipping is excluded from warranty coverage and will be assessed on a replacement. Consumable or hygienic items are not covered under warranty. Pictures and details of defects need to be provided. Issues outside of product defects will be non‑covered.

For your specific question: the Mobicushion‑L itself falls under the Mobi Cushions category and therefore carries a 6‑month warranty on the unit and pad. The battery is considered an accessory and is not covered under the warranty policy.

Actual Answer

PRODUCTS Water Therapy Systems

The Arctic Ice Classic– Cold Water Therapy Device with Large Back Pad for Treatment of Disability

THERAPEUTIC COLD WATER: The Arctic Ice cold therapy machine uses a continuous flow of cold water to provide reduction of swelling and inflammation for those with a disability.

PORTABLE AND QUIET: Designed for easy portability and storage, this compact ice therapy system has a quiet motor for use in the home or hospital.

DIGITAL CONTROLS: The built-in LCD screen and adjustable settings give you an easy way to control the treatment time and motor intensity as well as monitor water temperature. Convenient easy to read controls are at your fingertips.

EASY TO ADD ICE: The insulated walls of the AIS allow you to see the fill line easily as well as monitor the ice level in your machine. You’ll know right away when it’s time to refill the unit to keep the cool flowing.

BACK PAD: Included with your Ice Machine is a Back therapy pad which can be utilized for coverage of the back or large body area. The flexible pad and device have insulated tubing to keep temps cooler longer.

PRODUCT CODE: AISBACK

THERAPY INFO

WARRANTY

DESCRIPTION

The Arctic Ice is an ice therapy machine with a clear, insulated cooler basin and tubing, digital controls, and a multi-use therapy pad. The AIS has the ability to control the length of treatment.

Actual Answer

NERVESPA SILVER CONDUCTIVE GLOVE - HAND GARMENT SYSTEM INCLUDES DEVICE

Same technical specs as the Nerve Spa foot bath. Foot garment system is lightweight and highly portable. Touch screen device. Built-in Microprocessor. 7.83Hz, 80Hz, Symmetrical Biphasic Square, and Monophasic Waveform. Reinforced lead wires. Includes 1 Pair of Gloves. FDA Registered. Includes 24oz of conductive Spray.

PRODUCT CODE: NSGG10

THERAPY INFO

WARRANTY

DESCRIPTION

The Nerve Spa Pro is an Advanced Nerve and Neuropathy stimulator that utilizes a proprietary hybrid of electrotherapy including enhanced micro-current, galvanic, and TENS formulated into a proprietary specification—coupled with a monophasic waveform to target pain associated with early-onset peripheral neuropathy, as well other nerve-based pain conditions in the hands and feet.

Circumferential Nerve Stimulation Treatment
Surrounds the feet with a soothing and uniform level of current that relieves pain.

Damaged Nerve Repair
Increases the body's blood circulation, relaxes inflamed nerves, and works on a cellular level to stimulate the body's own healing mechanisms and repair damaged nerves.

60-90-Day, Drug-Free Treatment to Reduce Pain
Using the Nerve Spa over the course of 60 days or longer achieves an aggregate therapeutic effect that works to reverse and relieve.

Actual Answer

Soft-Touch Carbon Electrodes with cloth back (PMT gel / sometimes referred to alongside Tyco-style cloth gel) are reusable, self-adhesive TENS/EMS electrodes designed for good conductivity, durability, and comfort.

Key product details from PMT:

• Product type: Soft-Touch Carbon Electrodes, cloth back (PMT gel)
• Primary uses: Universal for most TENS units and EMS, interferential (IF), and Russian stimulation devices that use a standard pin-type connector.
• Backing: Resilient blue cloth/fabric backing with oval corners to help minimize edge curl.
• Connector: Pig-tail pin connector designed to fit standard pin-lead TENS/EMS cables.
• Conductor: Carbon conductive layer for even current dispersion.
• Resistance: 45 ohms of resistance.
• Self-adhesive gel: High-quality conductive gel layer designed for multiple uses (PMT gel; PMT also lists similar cloth-back electrodes with Tyco-style gel in the same family/category).
• Reusability: Average use is about 10–15 uses per pad (or more, depending on use time and care).
• Packaging: Resealable (air-tight) pouch to help keep gel moist and extend electrode life.
• Typical configurations:
  - 2.0" x 2.0" square
  - 2.0" round
  - 3" round
  - Butterfly 6" x 3.3" (one dual-wired/butterfly-style electrode)
  - Available in packs such as 10 packs of 4 electrodes per pack (or 10 packs of 1 for butterfly shape), depending on size and product code.
• Example product codes: FA3000, FA3360, FA2000 (codes vary by size and pack configuration).
• Spec highlights (representative):
  - Size example: 2.0" x 2.0" (approx. 5 cm x 5 cm)
  - Product weight: about 1 lb per bulk pack (varies by configuration)
  - Weight code: G (per PMT catalog)

Care and lifespan guidance from PMT:

• Lifespan: For best therapy results, PMT recommends replacing electrodes after the conductive gel starts to wear down, which typically occurs after about 10–15 uses per pad (depending on length of sessions and skin condition).
• To enhance electrode life and effectiveness:
  - After use, you can rub a small drop of water on the gel surface to help rehydrate it.
  - Always place electrodes back into the air-tight, resealable package immediately after use.
  - Use on clean, dry skin and avoid lotions or oils before application.

General performance notes:

• Designed to deliver great current dispersion and durability at a value price.
• Compatible with almost all TENS, EMS, IF, and Russian units that use pin-type connectors.
• Cloth backing is comfortable on the skin; foam-back Soft-Touch versions are also available separately if you need pads for moist or sweaty environments.

If you tell me what size or connector type your device uses, I can help you match the closest Soft-Touch Carbon cloth-back option in this family.

Actual Answer

Toilet Tilt – Heavy-Duty, Adjustable Assist Seat for Seniors at Home or in Medical Settings

Smooth Glide Motion: Silent, stepless lifting mechanism ensures gentle and stable elevation for all mobility levels.

Spacious & Strong Build: Wide seat and armrests, crafted with a high-strength plastic frame to support up to 200 kg.

Rechargeable Battery: Built-in battery. This Toilet Tilt can be used while plugged in for continuous use or battery-powered (will need to be charged when the battery runs out).

Custom Comfort & Easy Assembly: The height-adjustable seat features a friendly setup, and additional wheels are available for easy installation.

Whisper-Quiet Motion: Stepless lift technology with an adjustable height of 0-11”.

Always Operational: Backup battery guarantees lift function during power outages—no interruptions to safety or routine.

Easy Setup & Custom Fit: Hassle-free assembly, plus adjustable seat height for personalized comfort.

Product Code: TTLift

Description: Elevate your daily routine with this smart electric toilet lift chair, thoughtfully designed to support seniors and individuals with mobility challenges. Its whisper-quiet lift mechanism and wide, ergonomic design offer safe, stable assistance with every use. Equipped with reliable backup battery power, it delivers consistent performance at home or in medical settings. Easy to set up and adjust, this chair blends advanced features with practical comfort to restore independence and confidence. Designed to promote comfort, safety, and independence, this advanced electric toilet lift chair offers reliable support for seniors and individuals with limited mobility, whether at home or in a healthcare environment. Its stepless lifting mechanism allows for smooth angle adjustments up to 11 inches, making sit-to-stand transitions easier and more comfortable. With a weight capacity of up to 200 kg (440.92 lbs), the Toilet Tilt accommodates a wide range of users. Its universal design ensures effortless transfers, delivering dependable assistance for those with mobility challenges.

Actual Answer

The NerveBeam LED Light Therapy Wrap is a high-powered red and infrared light therapy device designed to deliver low-level but intense light energy into the body for pain relief, circulation support, and tissue warming.

Key details:
- Product name: NerveBeam LED Light Therapy Wrap
- Product code: NBRT175 (also listed as NBRT175 PAIR for the 1-pair option)
- Device type: Flexible LED light therapy wrap for provider-guided clinic and home-use applications
- Wavelengths:
  - Red light: 660 nm (visible; most effective at the surface of the skin)
  - Infrared light: 830 nm and 850 nm (invisible; penetrates approximately 1.5 inches into tissue)
- LED count: 600 individual LED diodes
- Total power: 22,000 mW total output
- Thermal output: Reaches temperatures over 100°F
- Placement: Adjustable straps allow use anywhere on the body, including wraps for the feet
- Regulatory status: FDA registered device

Primary benefits and functions:
- Elevates tissue temperature
- Temporarily relieves minor muscle and joint pain
- Temporarily improves local blood circulation in targeted areas
- Promotes muscle relaxation and helps relieve stiffness
- Supports the body’s natural healing processes at a cellular level (within provider-guided use)

How it works (per PMT’s description):
- Red light (660 nm):
  - Operates in the long end of the visible spectrum (approximately 630–700 nm)
  - Targets surface tissues
  - Has an analgesic (pain-relieving) and anti-inflammatory effect at the surface level
- Infrared light (830 nm and 850 nm):
  - Invisible and penetrates deeper (about 1.5 inches)
  - Delivers heat into tissue to provide temporary pain relief
  - Helps promote relaxation of muscle tissue and reduction of stiffness

Use context:
- Designed for broad-surface treatment areas rather than small focal points
- Suitable for clinical environments and structured, provider-guided clinic-to-home routines
- All use should be determined by the supervising clinician and adapted to the treatment area, patient tolerance, and overall care plan
- Users should follow all device instructions and safety guidance in the NerveBeam User Manual

Specifications (from PMT):
- Product weight: 2.2 lbs
- Length: 32 cm
- Width: 23 cm
- Height: 4 cm

For full setup, operation, placement, safety, and ongoing use instructions, PMT directs users to review the NerveBeam User Manual and any related resources on their site.

Actual Answer

Nerve Target Roll-On Gel is a clinician-developed topical formulation designed for targeted therapeutic treatment of damaged nerves, intractable back pain, joint aches, and muscle spasms.

It is formulated to:
- SUPPORT TARGETED COOLING & SENSORY MODULATION using Menthol + Camphor
- SUPPORT LOCAL MICRO-CIRCULATION using Eucalyptus + Witch Hazel + Arnica
- PROMOTE RAPID TOPICAL ABSORPTION using a plant-based carrier system

Product information:
- Product Name: Nerve Target Roll-On Gel
- Product Code: NTROLL

Therapy description:
Localized musculoskeletal discomfort is commonly associated with transient inflammatory mediator signaling, altered peripheral sensory receptor activation, and microvascular circulation shifts. Nerve Target Roll-On Gel is a clinician-developed topical formulation designed to support sensory receptor modulation and localized circulation using counterirritant compounds and botanical extracts evaluated in controlled human research.*

Formulation strategy – convergent sensory & circulatory support:
This formulation integrates TRP-channel activators, vasomodulatory botanicals, and antioxidant-rich plant extracts studied for their roles in sensory signaling and localized comfort.

TRP Channel Sensory Modulation – Menthol:
Menthol activates TRPM8 receptors and has been evaluated in controlled human trials for topical cooling mechanisms and sensory modulation.

If you need details on ingredients, usage directions, or compatibility with other therapies or devices, please let me know what specifically you’d like to review about Nerve Target Roll-On Gel.

Actual Answer

Cryotherapy and Hot Water Treatment System – Water Circulating Device by Aqua Relief

This is a hot and cold water therapy circulation system designed for post-surgical recovery and for treating chronic or acute disabilities. It is used with PMT water therapy pads/wraps (universal, knee, or shoulder pads depending on the model selected).

How this hot and cold therapy unit is used:
- Hot and cold water is quietly circulated through a leak-free 52" hose to the therapy pad, providing full hot or cold coverage to the body area.
- For cold therapy: Add ice and water; the cryo-cool system circulates cold water for up to 5 hours to help reduce edema and swelling.
- For heat therapy: The heating system warms up in less than 10 minutes and can provide the desired heat level.

Universal wrap / pads:
- The universal wrap measures 11" x 11" and includes a 3" x 39" Velcro strap.
- It can be used on various areas such as the hip, ankle, wrist, shoulder, leg, or elbow.
- Additional pad designs (such as knee and shoulder pads) are sold separately, depending on the specific product code.

Key therapy features:
- Long-duration low-level heat can be used for extended treatment for individuals with disabilities.
- Ice (cold) therapy with cold water is used to reduce edema and swelling.
- Indication for use: In-hospital setting to treat chronic and acute disabilities.

Product variants and codes:
- With Universal Pad: PRODUCT CODE – ARSwTPU
- With Back Pad: PRODUCT CODE – ARSwBACK
- With Shoulder Pad: PRODUCT CODE – ARSwSHOULDER
- With Knee Pad: PRODUCT CODE – ARSwKNEE

Important use notes:
- Always keep water above the fill line when operating the unit.
- Not for commercial/continuous use – pump life expectancy is approximately 1700 hours (where specified in the product description).

Compatibility and accessories:
- PMT also offers a Cold Water Therapy Coupling Converter that allows many existing cryotherapy units to be used with PMT brand water therapy pads. These quick-connect/disconnect couplings can replace existing fittings to improve compatibility and help prevent leaks.
- PMT water therapy pads can be used with both hot and cold circulating units when properly connected.

For full product details, model selection, and purchasing options, you can browse the Water Therapy Systems category on the PMT website: https://paintechnology.com/category

Actual Answer

The iTENS Gen 2 Docking Station and Charging Cord (PRODUCT CODE: ITCGRCARD) is an accessory designed specifically for the iTENS Gen 2 device. It serves as the charging base and power connection for your unit.

From the PMT information provided:

• Product Name: iTENS Gen 2 docking station and charging cord  
• Category: Electrotherapy Supplies  
• Product Code: ITCGRCARD  
• Description: The iTENS Gen 2 docking station and charging cord  
• Specifications:  
  • Product Weight (lbs): 1  
  • Length (cm): 1

For lead wires, PMT offers several universal TENS lead wire options that are compatible with most TENS/EMS electrodes and many electrotherapy devices:

1) Universal TENS Lead Wires – Premium Grade (PRODUCT CODE: P-LWT)
• Extra strong construction: about twice the thickness of standard wires, with more copper for durability and higher conduction.  
• Universal fit: Standard shielded plastic female right-angle plug, FDA-compliant, fits standard 2 mm pigtail electrode/pad connectors.  
• Length: 48 inches.  
• One pair per set: 2 leads to connect up to 4 electrodes.  
• Description: High quality Premium Tiger lead wires composed of 24 copper strands for exceptional wear and tear resistance. Typically last 1–2 years for heavy users.  
• Benefit: Worn-out lead wires can cause shocks or jolts; replacing them supports optimal therapy results.  
• Packaging: Sold in packs of two (1 pair).  
• Size/Model: Premium Grade.  
• Product Weight (lbs): 1.

2) Universal TENS Lead Wires – Standard Grade (PRODUCT CODE: LWS)
• Universal fit: Standard shielded plastic female right-angle plug, FDA-compliant.  
• Standard 2 mm pins: Fit standard 2 mm pigtail electrode pad connectors.  
• Length: 48 inches.  
• One pair per set: 2 leads to connect up to 4 electrodes.  
• Description: High quality standard lead wires composed of 12 copper strands. Typical lifespan is about 6–12 months for heavy users, depending on use.  
• Note: Worn-out lead wires can cause shocks or jolts; periodic replacement is recommended for best therapy performance.  
• Packaging: Sold in packs of two (1 pair).  
• Size/Model: Standard Grade.  
• Product Weight (lbs): 1.

3) Electrotherapy Device Leadwires (PRODUCT CODE: ET99090101)
• Connector type: Male pin-wire connection (long thin cylinder approximately 0.4 inches long) compatible with any electrode sold on Healiohealth.  
• Lifespan: Typically 1–6 months depending on device settings and usage.  
• Purpose: Periodic replacement is part of normal electrotherapy device upkeep.  
• Description: Standard lead wires for electrotherapy devices.  
• Specifications:  
  • Product Weight (lbs): 1  
  • Width (cms): 2  
  • Length – Inches: 2  
  • Weight Code: G

If you’d like, you can visit paintechnology.com and search the product codes (ITCGRCARD, P-LWT, LWS, or ET99090101) to see full product pages and purchasing options.

Actual Answer

PMT Medical Ultima OTC TENS Unit

Category: Electrotherapy Devices • TENS

Description:
The PMT Medical Ultima OTC TENS Unit is an over-the-counter TENS (Transcutaneous Electrical Nerve Stimulation) device designed for pain relief using safe, drug-free electrical nerve stimulation applied through electrodes on the skin.

Typical Package Includes (based on standard PMT TENS packages):
- TENS device unit
- Lead wires
- 1 pack of 4 electrodes
- Batteries (or power source as specified)
- Carrying case
- User manual / instruction booklet

Key Features (as consistent with PMT Ultima-series TENS units):
- Dual-channel output (allows use of 2 or 4 electrodes at once)
- Multiple operation modes (such as Normal, Burst, Modulation – exact modes may vary by model)
- Adjustable parameters including:
  - Pulse amplitude (intensity)
  - Pulse width
  - Pulse frequency
- Timer function with selectable treatment durations (commonly in preset minute ranges)
- Designed for treatment of pain via surface electrodes placed at or near the site of pain.

Intended Use:
The Ultima OTC TENS unit is intended to help relieve pain and discomfort associated with a variety of conditions where TENS therapy is appropriate and where an OTC TENS device is permitted by your healthcare provider and local regulations.

Important Notes:
- Always follow the instructions in the user manual that comes with the specific Ultima OTC device you have.
- Use only as directed and consult a healthcare professional regarding whether TENS therapy is appropriate for your specific condition.

For detailed specifications, features, and documentation, you can browse PMT’s TENS products and manuals here:
https://paintechnology.com/category
and the Resources Page:
https://paintechnology.com/resources

Actual Answer

Extra Large Reusable Ice Cubes (2.6” Square) - BPA Free Food Grade Plastic - Ice Therapy System – Includes 6 Cubes/pack

These extra-large reusable ice cubes are designed for both cold therapy machines and general cooling uses like drink coolers and drink dispensers.

Key features:
- LONG-LASTING COLD: These reusable plastic cubes are designed to stay cold longer than standard ice cubes. They are easier and more convenient than refilling ice trays, and when kept in your freezer, they’re always ready to go.
- EXTRA LARGE SIZE: Each cube is 2.6" square, which is three to four times bigger than typical 1-inch reusable ice cubes. The larger size helps them stay cold longer, making them ideal for large containers, pitchers, and cold therapy machines.
- COLD THERAPY UNITS: These cubes (often called Cold Cubes) were originally created for use with cryotherapy units such as the Arctic Ice System or Polar Vortex. They are designed to stay cold for longer periods, helping extend the usable time of a cold therapy machine before you need to refill ice.
- BPA-FREE PLASTIC: Made with BPA-Free plastic and filled with clean water, so whether you’re using them in a drink or a machine, there is no contamination risk in case of breakage.
- 6 CUBE PACK: Each box includes 6 individual extra-large cubes. When using an ice therapy machine or large drink container, you can alternate between cubes in the unit and cubes in the freezer so you always have cold cubes ready.

Intended uses:
- Housewares and Outdoor Activities:
  - Hard or soft coolers
  - Punch bowls
  - Water pitchers
- Water Therapy Systems:
  - Use to help assist in ice rotations for cold therapy/ice therapy machines

Product details:
- Product Name: Extra Large Reusable Ice Cubes (Cold Cubes)
- Size: 2.6" square
- Product Code: CCB2
- Product Weight (lbs): 5
- Length (cm): 28
- Width (cm): 8
- Height (cm): 15
- HCPCS Code: NA

These cubes are designed as an accessory for cold/ice therapy systems and for general cooling; they are not part of a TENS unit itself, but can be used alongside water therapy systems in your overall pain management setup.

Actual Answer

ADVANCED RUSSIAN STIM + INTERFERENTIAL BY StimForce is a dual-modality electrotherapy device that combines Russian Stimulation with Interferential (IFC) therapy.

Key PMT details:

• **Product Name:** ADVANCED RUSSIAN STIM + INTERFERENTIAL BY StimForce. 2-channel, 2500Hz/4000Hz  
• **Product Code:** R3500  
• **Modalities:**  
  - Russian Stim  
  - Interferential (IFC)

• **Frequencies:**  
  - Russian Stim operates at **2500 Hz**  
  - Interferential operates at **4000 Hz**

• **Design & Operation:**  
  - **Dual Modality:** Russian Stim combined with Interferential in one unit  
  - **Digital:** Digital LCD display  
  - **Portable:** AC/DC adaptor and battery operable

• **Therapy Description – Interferential (IFC):**  
  - Used as a **more therapeutic form of TENS** as the high frequency drives the current deep into the tissue.  
  - Interferential stimulation provides powerful and deep stimulation by using **two alternating currents of different frequencies** that work together to produce an interferential current.  
  - Intended for **chronic pain relief therapy** and **anti-inflammatory-based treatments**.

• **Therapy Description – Russian Stim:**  
  - Russian stimulation is commonly used to assist a muscle in **strengthening more quickly following injury or surgery**.  
  - Uses **high frequencies** to provide electrical stimulation to muscle groups to aid in delivering **muscle mass building**.  
  - Russian Stimulation has been known to be used by **Olympic athletes** to drive bulk muscle building results.

• **Russian Stim Technical Detail (from PMT entry):**  
  - Wave Form: **Biphasic Square Pulse**  
  - Amplitude: **Adjustable, 0–60 mA peak at 1000 ohm load each channel, ~1.0 mA/step**

This device is categorized under Electrotherapy Devices • Russian Stimulators and is designed to both help reduce chronic pain & inflammation (via Interferential) and support muscle strengthening and spasm relief (via Russian Stim). For purchasing, availability, or full specifications, you can visit https://paintechnology.com or contact PMT directly.

Actual Answer

Soft-Touch Foam Electrodes from PMT use an Ag silver conductor as part of their construction.

For example, the Soft-Touch Carbon Electrodes Foam back (PMT gel) include:

• Product names:
  - Soft-Touch Foam Electrodes (PMT gel) 2.0 x 2.0 (Product code: FAF2020)
  - Soft-Touch Foam Electrodes (PMT gel) 2.0" x 2.0" Round (Product code: FAF2000)

• Key feature related to silver:
  - Ag silver conductor with great performance

• Additional features:
  - Foam backing that can be used in moist environments and does not absorb sweat like cloth
  - Ultra tight pigtail connector with a snug plug
  - Prewired and reusable electrode
  - Self-adhesive, reusable, disposable electrodes with a pigtail pin connector
  - Oval corners to help minimize edge curl
  - Designed for great dispersion, durability, and value pricing

• Therapy compatibility (effective for use with):
  - TENS
  - EMS
  - Interferential
  - Micro current
  - Galvanic generators

• Pack/size details (FAF2020 and FAF2000 lines):
  - 2.0 x 2.0: 10 packs of 4 electrodes per pack
  - 2.0 Round: 10 packs of 4 electrodes per pack

• Specifications example (FAF2020):
  - Size: 2.0 x 2.0
  - Product Weight: 1 lb
  - Width: 5 cm
  - Height: 3 cm

The main information PMT provides about silver in its electrodes is that these Soft-Touch Foam Electrodes incorporate an Ag silver conductor to support high performance in electrotherapy applications, while remaining reusable, self-adhesive, and compatible with a wide range of stimulation devices.

Actual Answer

A Transcutaneous Electrical Nerve Stimulation (TENS) unit is a small, battery-powered device that helps relieve pain by sending low-voltage electrical pulses through electrode pads placed on the skin. These gentle electrical impulses stimulate the nerves beneath the skin and can help reduce the way your body perceives pain.

For home use, a good TENS unit generally offers:
- Adjustable intensity levels
- Multiple modes/programs
- Dual channels (so you can treat two areas or use four electrodes at once)
- Rechargeable batteries
- Easy-to-use controls and a clear display

Many PMT home TENS units are dual channel units (up to 130 mA) with multiple modes and waveforms, timer options (for example 15–90 minutes plus continuous), and features like a compliance lock. Your typical kit may include:
- The TENS device
- User manual and Quick Start Guide
- One pack of 2" x 2" square electrodes
- AA charger
- Two pairs of lead wires
- Two rechargeable Li-ion batteries
- A soft carrying case
- An instruction card

Regarding electrodes:
- PMT Soft-Touch electrodes are self-adhesive, high quality gel electrodes with about 45 ohms of resistance.
- They are reusable for about 10–15 (or more) uses per pad, depending on use time.
- They are universal for TENS, EMS, IF, and Russian units that use standard pin-type connectors.
- They come in various shapes and sizes (e.g., 2.0" x 2.0" squares, 2.0" or 3.0" round, and 6" x 3.3" butterfly shapes), with either cloth or foam backing.
- Foam-backed versions are designed not to absorb sweat and can be used in moist environments.

Electrode care and lifespan:
- Electrode life-span and effectiveness can be enhanced by rubbing a small drop of water on the pad after use.
- Always keep your pads in the airtight, resealable pouch directly after use.
- For best therapy results, electrodes should be replaced once the conductive gel starts to wear down, which typically occurs after about 10–15 uses per pad.
- Oval corners help minimize edge curl, and the pig-tail pin connector is designed for a snug, secure connection.

If by “heating electrodes” you mean warmth from use: the PMT information describes standard electrical gel electrodes for TENS and other electrotherapy (not heat-producing pads). Any warmth you feel typically comes from skin contact and circulation changes, not from a heating element in the electrode itself.

Actual Answer

Womens Health

Softcycle Pelvic Floor Stimulator by PMT -Electronic Kegel Exerciser with Probe for Pelvic Floor strengthening and bladder strength for Women and Men - Vaginal

KEGEL EXERCISES: an easy contraction-and-relaxation exercise that makes the muscles under the uterus, bladder, and bowel (large intestine) stronger. Help both men and women who have problems with a weak bladder. Soft Cycle was designed to perform a perfect electronic Kegel Exercise. Low frequency currents used in the Soft Cycle increase the blood flow to the muscles increasing the resistance to fatigue and reducing the involuntary contractions of the bladder.

STRENGTHEN MUSCLES: Stronger pelvic floor muscles can help bladder leakage of small amounts of urine during sneezing, coughing and other activities which increase the pressure in the abdomen. Stronger bladder muscles can also help an overactive bladder that causes leakage of large amounts of urine at unexpected times, including sleep. The Soft Cycle offers specifically engineered programs for both of these types of issues.

PROPRIETARY SETTINGS: Two pre-set Programs and a specially designed probe allow for advanced performance with effective results. Strengthen the pelvic floor muscles and achieve postpartum recovery.

PMT also offers a full Womens Health product category that includes:
- Softcycle Pelvic Floor Stimulator options
- Portable Vaginal Rejuvenation Wand by SoftCycle VT, Red & Blue LED Light Anti-inflammatory Device, Woman Gynecological Vaginitis Treatment, Female Home Care Device, Menopause Device, Women Health Care
- Softcycle Wireless Pelvic Floor Stimulator with Soft Purple Probe, Rechargeable Kegel Exerciser, Multiple Modes & Adjustable Intensity for Muscle Training, Compact & Travel-Friendly Design
- CAREss Cup Pulse (Vibrating Attachment)
- CAREss Cups (5-Pack)
- Breast Implant Stabilizer Band by PMT, Post Surgery Breast Augmentation and Reduction Strap, Chest Belt, Breast Support Bandage, One Size Fits All
- Extender (2-pack)

For full details, specifications, and all Womens Health products, you can browse the Womens Health category on the PMT website: https://paintechnology.com/category

Actual Answer

Soft-Touch electrodes are PMT's premium reusable line and include the 1PK gel electrodes. They feature a non-porous tricot backing, a conductive wire-mount layer, a high-quality carbon layer, and an extra-thick gel, with a snug pigtail connector. Oval corners help minimize edge curl, and every pad uses latex-free USA gel in a resealable bag to stay fresh.

These electrodes are designed to work with a wide range of stimulation devices, including TENS, EMS, interferential (IF), microcurrent, and galvanic units, as long as they use the compatible pin-style/pigtail connector.

General characteristics of Soft-Touch / PMT gel electrodes:
- Self-adhesive, high-quality gel electrodes
- Reusable, typically around 10–15 or more uses per pad (depending on use and care)
- Compatible with most TENS, EMS, IF, Russian, and similar electrotherapy units that use standard pin-type connectors
- Latex-free gel and backing materials, with good flexibility and skin contact
- Packaged in a resealable pouch to keep the gel fresh and extend life

Care and lifespan guidance from PMT:
- The life-span and effectiveness can often be enhanced by lightly rehydrating the gel surface (PMT notes that a small drop of water rubbed on the pad after use can help in some of their electrode lines).
- Always return the electrodes to the airtight, resealable pouch immediately after use.
- For best results, replace electrodes after the conductive gel starts to wear down or they no longer adhere properly, typically around 10–15 uses per pad.

If you need exact size, shape, or packaging quantity for a specific 1PK gel electrode variant (for example, square, round, butterfly, or dual-wire), you can view the full electrode lineup here:
https://paintechnology.com/category/electrodes

For purchase details or to confirm compatibility with your specific unit, you can also contact Customer Service at info@paintechnology.com or 1-800-239-7880.

Actual Answer

Soft-Touch Carbon Electrodes with foam back (PMT gel) are PMT’s premium reusable electrodes designed for TENS and other electrotherapy treatments.

Here are the key details from PMT:

• Product name: Soft-Touch Carbon Electrodes Foam back (PMT gel)
• Product codes:
  • FAF2000 – 2.0” Round (foam back)
  • FAF2020 – 2.0” x 2.0” square (foam back)

• Sizes / packaging:
  • 2.0” x 2.0” – 10 packs of 4 electrodes per pack (FAF2020)
  • 2.0” Round – 10 packs of 4 electrodes per pack (FAF2000)

• Backing & construction:
  • Foam backing that can be used in moist environments
  • Foam backing will not absorb sweat like a cloth electrode
  • Soft-Touch™ electrodes are self-adhesive, reusable, disposable electrodes
  • Use a pigtail pin connector with an ultra-tight pigtail and snug plug
  • Oval corners help minimize edge curl
  • Carbon-based electrode with Ag silver conductor for great performance

• Performance & compatibility:
  • Deliver great current dispersion and durability
  • Effective for use with:
    • TENS
    • EMS
    • Interferential
    • Microcurrent
    • Galvanic generators
  • Designed as value-priced, high-performance electrodes

• Reusability:
  • Pre-wired and reusable
  • Packaged in resealable pouches to help keep the PMT gel fresh

• Specifications (representative for the 2.0" Round size, FAF2000):
  • Product Weight (lbs): 1
  • Width (cm): 5
  • Height (cm): 3
  • Size: 2.0" Round
  • Weight Code: G

• Specifications (representative for the 2.0" x 2.0" size, FAF2020):
  • Product Weight (lbs): 1
  • Width (cm): 5
  • Height (cm): 3
  • Size: 2.0" x 2.0"
  • Weight Code: G

In summary, Soft-Touch Carbon Electrodes foam-back (PMT gel) are self-adhesive, reusable, foam-backed electrodes with a carbon/Ag silver conductor and pigtail pin connector, designed for reliable performance and comfort across TENS and other electrotherapy devices, especially useful in moist or sweaty environments because the foam does not absorb moisture like cloth.

Actual Answer

Orthopedic Neck Travel Pillow with Sleep Hood by SkyPillow, Prevent Neck Cramps and Pain. Patented Memory Foam Pillow, Washable Cover, Sleep Hood, Includes Carrying Case (One Size Fits Most)

A BETTER NECK PILLOW: Many travel pillows aren't firm enough to properly support the neck and end up caving in to even the slightest amount of pressure. This does very little to actually help relieve neck pain or provide comfort during travel. The SkyPillow corrects this with sturdy memory foam construction that cradles the neck that reduces strain and keeps the head in an upright position.

SIZING: The Skypillow Hoodie comes in one size, with an adjustable strap and a height of 12 centimeters to ensure that you can receive the right relief for you. Fits circumference: 12.5” – 16” circumference.

ULTRA BREATHABLE FABRIC: The SkyPillow is constructed with a coolpass fabric that allows air to pass through the neck pillow. This breathable and lightweight fabric makes for a super comfortable pillow as the flow of air prevents the user from feeling overly constricted. During long periods of sitting or travel, it can be difficult to have something wrapped around your neck when the heat and sweat build. Keep your neck cool and comfy with the SkyPillow!

Electrotherapy probe PR-04A – Urinary Incontinence Aid Uro Probe

Product Category:
• Electrotherapy Supplies → Probes and clips

Product Name:
• Electrotherapy probe PR-04A – Urinary Incontinence Aid Uro Probe

Product Code:
• PR04A

Therapy Info / Indications:
• Designed for treating urinary incontinence
• Indications: urinary incontinence, urinary problem

Description:
• Urinary Incontinence Aid Uro Probe is specially designed for treating incontinence.
• Completely fits with the body curve and completely adheres to the treatment region.
• Helps ease muscle for better contraction.
• Effectively equipped for use with various incontinence and pelvic muscle stimulators.
• High safety and steadiness, with no over-stimulation on the treatment region.

Usage Requirements:
• These probes must be used in tandem with an EMS (electrical muscle stimulation) device in order to attain desired results.
• The Uro probe is specially equipped for incontinence stimulators, womancare devices, or other pelvic muscle stimulators whose output is less than 0.25 W/cm².

Specifications:
• Product Weight (lbs): 1
• Model: Vaginal Medium

If you need details on compatible EMS or pelvic muscle stimulator devices, you can browse PMT’s product catalog at https://paintechnology.com/category or contact customer service for guidance on pairing this probe with the correct device.

Actual Answer

Soft-Touch 1PK Gel Electrodes from Pain Management Technologies (PMT) are part of PMT’s Soft-Touch reusable electrode line and are designed for use with most TENS, EMS, interferential (IF), and Russian stimulation units that use a standard pin-type connector.

Key things to know:

• **Compatibility**: 
  - Universal for almost all TENS units and EMS devices, IF, and Russian units that use a **pin-type connector** (typically 2 mm).

• **Construction & Backing** (Soft-Touch family features):
  - Self-adhesive, high-quality **PMT gel**.
  - Available with **carbon** and **blue cloth** backing.
  - **Oval corners** help minimize edge curl.
  - Designed to provide **great dispersion** and durability.

• **Typical Use & Lifespan**:
  - **Reusable**: generally about **10–15 uses per pad**, depending on length of each session and skin prep.
  - For best therapy results, electrodes should be **replaced once the conductive gel starts to wear down** or they no longer adhere well.

• **Care & Handling**:
  - After each use, you can **rub a small drop of water** on the gel surface to help enhance electrode life-span and keep the gel hydrated.
  - Always store electrodes **immediately after use** in their **resealable, air-tight pouch** to prevent drying out and contamination.

• **Connection Style**:
  - Uses a **pigtail pin connector**, compatible with standard pin-lead TENS/EMS cables.

• **Intended Use**:
  - Designed as **reusable, disposable gel pads** for delivering electrical stimulation from your TENS/EMS/IF/Russian unit to the skin.

If you tell me which device you’re using (for example, Ultima 5, Ultima 20, etc.), I can confirm connector compatibility and suggest what size/shape might fit your treatment area best based on the Soft-Touch line.

Splitter Hose by Thermacycle – Connect Pillow Pad and Mattress Pad at the Same Time.

Actual Answer

Mild redness after using a TENS unit can be normal and is often caused by increased circulation, pressure from the electrode, or mild skin sensitivity. It should typically fade shortly after removing the pads. To reduce skin irritation: Make sure your skin is clean and dry before use. Avoid placing pads on irritated or damaged skin. Rotate pad placement between treatments. Reduce treatment intensity if the sensation feels uncomfortable. Replace electrodes when the gel becomes old or irritating.

A TENS unit should never be painful. If you experience sharp, burning, or uncomfortable sensations, reduce the intensity or check that the electrode pads are properly positioned and making good contact with your skin.\n\nUseful Resources & Links:\n🛠️ [Electrotherapy Device Troubleshooting Guide](https://paintechnology.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/pdf/Electrotherapy%20device%20TROUBLESHOOTING.docx)"

}

Actual Answer

In many cases, yes, TENS can be used with metal implants, such as joint replacements or surgical hardware. The electrical stimulation from a TENS unit typically does not affect non-electronic metal implants. However, you should check with your healthcare provider before use, especially if the implant is near the treatment area or if you have any type of electronic implant (such as a pacemaker, defibrillator, or nerve stimulator).

  "acceptableAnswers": [
    "TENS can help with multiple conditions such as Sciatica, Plantar Fasciitis, Arthritis, Knee Pain, Shoulder Pain, Neck Pain, Tennis Elbow, Carpal Tunnel, Fibromyalgia, Neuropathy, Muscle Spasms, Menstrual Cramps, and Post-Surgical Pain.",
    "TENS can help with multiple conditions such as Sciatica, Plantar Fasciitis, Arthritis, Knee Pain, Shoulder Pain, Neck Pain, Tennis Elbow, Carpal Tunnel, Fibromyalgia, Neuropathy, Muscle Spasms, Menstrual Cramps, and Post-Surgical Pain.\n\nUseful Resources & Links:\n📄 [Microcurrent Cheatsheet v1.1](https://paintechnology.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/pdf/Microcurrent-Cheatsheet-v1.1.pdf)\n📄 [Tips on using Microcurrent](https://paintechnology.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/pdf/Tips%20on%20using%20a%20micro%20current%20device.doc)"
  ],

    "input\\qa\\Key - VA TENS Unit Bot.csv",
    "input\\examples\\predefined-answers-node.js"
  ]
}

Actual Answer

TENS (Transcutaneous Electrical Nerve Stimulation) is used for many types of acute and chronic pain. It does not cure the underlying condition, but may help make pain more manageable.

According to the information available, TENS may provide relief for:

• Back pain: back ache, low back pain, lumbago, whole back pain, lumbosacral pain, sprains/strains, thoracodynia, facet syndrome, IVD (disc) syndrome, intercostal neuralgia, radiculitis

• Arthritis and joint pain: osteoarthritis, rheumatoid arthritis, cervical arthrosis, arthritis, aching joints, synovitis, gonalgia (knee pain), hip pain (coxalgia), knee pain, ankle sprains, wrist pain

• Muscle and soft-tissue pain: muscular strains, myalgia, rheumatism, sports injuries, ankle sprains, muscular pain, torticollis, epicondylitis (tennis elbow), sprains/strains in various areas, passive stretch pain

• Nerve-related pain: neuralgia, trigeminal neuralgia, peripheral nerve injuries, diabetic peripheral neuropathy, causalgia, sciatica, ischialgia, phantom limb pain, shingles (herpes zoster–related pain)

• Head and neck pain: migraine headaches, cluster headaches, suboccipital headaches, TMJ syndrome, dental disorders, whiplash, spondylosis, torticollis, head and neck pain conditions listed above

• Systemic or generalized pain conditions: bursitis, Raynaud's syndrome, multiple sclerosis–related pain, cancer-related pain (for pain management, not treatment of cancer itself)

• Abdominal and pelvic pain: dysmenorrhea (menstrual pain), menses pain, labor pain, postoperative abdominal pain, diverticulosis-related pain, some types of post-surgery pain (as directed by a provider)

• Post-surgical and trauma-related pain: post-surgery pain, postoperative pain, fractures (pain management), whiplash and neck injury, some traumatic injuries and after surgery pain, as directed.

TENS is described as a drug-free option for managing both chronic pain (like arthritis or long-term back pain) and acute pain (such as post-surgical pain, sports injuries, sprains/strains).

Always follow the directions provided with your specific TENS unit and any instructions from your healthcare provider. If you have a pacemaker, cardiac condition, are pregnant, or need to place electrodes on the head or neck, you should consult your doctor before using TENS.

Actual Answer

It watches your connection for you. If contact weakens, "CH1" and/or "CH2" flashes on the display. After 3 seconds of poor contact, intensity automatically drops to 60%. Actual Answer

If only one pad seems to be producing a sensation, the issue is usually related to pad contact, connections, or the electrode itself. Try these steps: Check the pad connection: Make sure both lead wires are firmly connected to the TENS unit and electrode pads. Test the pads: Swap the pads or cables to see if the issue follows the pad or the channel. Replace the pad: A worn or dried-out electrode may not conduct properly. Improve the skin contact: Clean and dry the skin, then reapply the pads firmly. Check the channel settings: Make sure both channels are turned on if your unit has multiple channels.

A Transcutaneous Electrical Nerve Stimulation (TENS) unit is a small, battery-powered device that helps relieve pain by sending low-voltage electrical pulses through electrode pads placed on the skin.     "To get the best results from your TENS unit, focus on proper placement, comfortable settings, and consistent use. Tips to maximize relief: Place pads correctly: Position electrodes around the painful area or along the related nerve pathways as recommended. Use a comfortable intensity: The sensation should feel strong but comfortable - not painful or irritating. Adjust settings: Try different modes, pulse rates, and durations to find what works best for you. Use fresh electrodes - Good pad contact helps deliver consistent stimulation. Keep skin clean and dry: Remove oils, lotions, and sweat before applying pads. Use consistently: Regular sessions may provide better results than occasional use."

  "source": "qa",
  "sourceRef": [
    "input\\qa\\Key - VA TENS Unit Bot.csv",
    "input\\examples\\predefined-answers-node.js"
  ]
}

Actual Answer

To maximize pain relief with a TENS unit in everyday language, focus on four basics: pad placement, intensity, settings, and consistency.

1) Get pad placement close (but not on) the pain
- Put the sticky pads on clean, dry skin.
- Place them so the painful area is either:
  - Between the pads, or
  - Just under and around the pads.
- Don’t put pads:
  - On the front or side of your neck
  - On your head
  - Over your eyes
  - Directly over your heart

2) Use a strong but comfortable intensity
- Turn the unit on with intensity at zero.
- Slowly turn it up until you clearly feel the tingling or pulsing.
- The goal: **strong, noticeable, but not painful.**
- If it hurts, feels like “grabbing,” or makes muscles jump too hard, turn it down.

3) Try different frequencies (speeds) for your type of pain
- For **ongoing/chronic pain** (pain you’ve had for months or years):
  - Many people like **lower speeds** (around 1–10 Hz).
  - These may help your body release natural pain-relieving chemicals like endorphins.
- For **sudden or more intense pain**:
  - Many people like **higher speeds** (around 50–120 Hz).
  - These may help “block” pain signals more quickly.
- There is **no single best setting for everyone**. Your “best” setting is the one that feels good and eases your pain the most.

4) Give it time and be consistent
- Sessions are often **20–30 minutes**, but follow your device’s manual.
- Some people feel relief **while** it’s on; others notice relief that continues for **a while after** they turn it off.
- You may need to try **several sessions** and tweak settings before you find what works best for you.

5) Adjust when it isn’t helping
If you don’t feel much relief:
- Move the pads slightly closer to or around the painful area.
- Try a **different speed (frequency)**:
  - If you were using low, try a higher setting, or vice versa.
- Make sure the intensity is high enough to feel clearly but still comfortable.
- Check that pads are sticking well and not dried out.

6) Safety reminders
- Do **not** use a TENS unit if you have a **demand-type pacemaker**.
- Do **not** put pads on the **front or side of the neck**.
- Do **not** use the unit while **driving or operating machinery**.
- If you are **pregnant**, or have **heart disease or epilepsy**, you should get medical advice before using any TENS nerve stimulator.
- Never use TENS to hide or cover up **new, undiagnosed pain** that might need urgent medical care.

7) What TENS can and can’t do
- TENS is meant to **help relieve pain**, not to directly **heal** the underlying problem.
- By easing pain, it may help you move more comfortably and stay active.

If you tell me what kind of pain you’re dealing with (for example, lower back, knee, shoulder, etc.) and which PMT device you’re using (like Ultima 5, Ultima 20, etc.), I can walk you through more specific, plain-language steps for settings and pad placement.

Actual Answer

TENS pads can lose their stickiness over time due to repeated use, moisture, oils, lotions, dirt, or improper storage. As the gel dries out or the pads are used, the pads may not make proper contact with the skin, which will reduce stimulation quality. Once you are noticing that your pads are sticking less, or are starting to fall off the skin, it is recommended to swap out your pads for new. To improve pad life: Clean and dry your skin before applying pads. Avoid using lotions, oils, or creams before treatment. Store pads on their backing sheet in a sealed bag or container. Replace pads when the gel becomes dry, dirty, or no longer adheres well.

If your TENS unit will not power on, the issue is usually related to the battery, charging, or a connection problem. TEST-00073INFORMALwhat are the theories behind how tens units work plsOK98825 ms

Expected

{
  "answer": "There are two leading theories that explain why TENS provides relief, and understanding them can help you get more out of your device. The first is the Gate Control Theory of Pain. It suggests that the electrical pulses from your TENS unit travel along nerve fibers faster than pain signals do, effectively closing a \"gate\" in your spinal cord so fewer pain signals reach your brain. This is generally associated with higher frequency settings and tends to provide faster, more immediate relief. The second is the Endorphin Release Theory, which suggests that certain settings, typically lower frequencies, encourage your body to release endorphins, its own natural pain-relieving chemicals. This effect tends to build more gradually and can last longer after your session ends. Many people find that combining both approaches, or simply experimenting with frequency over a few sessions, helps them find what works best for their body.",
  "acceptableAnswers": [
    "There are two leading theories that explain why TENS provides relief, and understanding them can help you get more out of your device. The first is the Gate Control Theory of Pain. It suggests that the electrical pulses from your TENS unit travel along nerve fibers faster than pain signals do, effectively closing a \"gate\" in your spinal cord so fewer pain signals reach your brain. This is generally associated with higher frequency settings and tends to provide faster, more immediate relief. The second is the Endorphin Release Theory, which suggests that certain settings, typically lower frequencies, encourage your body to release endorphins, its own natural pain-relieving chemicals. This effect tends to build more gradually and can last longer after your session ends. Many people find that combining both approaches, or simply experimenting with frequency over a few sessions, helps them find what works best for their body."
  ],
  "behavior": "ANSWER_FROM_SOURCE",
  "intent": null,
  "source": "qa",
  "sourceRef": [
    "input\\qa\\Key - VA TENS Unit Bot.csv",
    "input\\examples\\predefined-answers-node.js"
  ]
}

Actual Answer

There are two leading theories that explain why TENS provides relief, and understanding them can help you get more out of your device. The first is the Gate Control Theory of Pain. It suggests that the electrical pulses from your TENS unit travel along nerve fibers faster than pain signals do, effectively closing a "gate" in your spinal cord so fewer pain signals reach your brain. This is generally associated with higher frequency settings and tends to provide faster, more immediate relief. The second is the Endorphin Release Theory, which suggests that certain settings, typically lower frequencies, encourage your body to release endorphins, its own natural pain-relieving chemicals. This effect tends to build more gradually and can last longer after your session ends. Many people find that combining both approaches, or simply experimenting with frequency over a few sessions, helps them find what works best for their body.

TEST-00078MULTI_INTENTWhy does my unit keep turning off Also, what accessories should I buy with a TENS unit?PARTIAL845977 ms

Expected

{
  "answer": [
    "If your TENS unit keeps shutting off, it is usually caused by a power, connection, or safety feature issue. Common issues include: Low battery: Replace or recharge the batteries to ensure the unit has enough power. Automatic Shutoff Timer: Many TENS units turn off automatically after a set treatment time. Loose connections: Check that the lead wires are fully connected to the unit and electrode pads. Poor pad contact: If the pads lose contact with the skin, some units may pause or stop stimulation. Damaged cables or electrodes: Worn pads or faulty wires can interrupt the connection. Intensity set too high: Some devices may stop if they detect an issue with the electrical connection.",
    "The most useful accessories depend on how often you use your TENS unit, but these are the most common add-ons: Extra electrode pads, Different Pad Sizes, Replacement Lead Wires, Carrying Case, Rechargable batteries or charging cable, Electrode storage bag or container."
  ],
  "behavior": "ANSWER_ALL_INTENTS",
  "intent": null,
  "source": "qa_pair",
  "sourceRef": [
    "input\\qa\\Key - VA TENS Unit Bot.csv",
    "input\\qa\\Key - VA TENS Unit Bot.csv"
  ]
}

Actual Answer

If your TENS unit keeps turning off, it is usually caused by a power, connection, or safety feature issue.

Common reasons include:
• Low battery: Replace or recharge the batteries to ensure the unit has enough power.
• Automatic shutoff timer: Many TENS units turn off automatically after a set treatment time.
• Loose connections: Check that the lead wires are fully connected to the unit and electrode pads.
• Poor pad contact: If the pads lose contact with the skin, some units may pause or stop stimulation.
• Damaged cables or electrodes: Worn pads or faulty wires can interrupt the connection.
• Intensity set too high: Some devices may stop if they detect an issue with the electrical connection.

If your TENS unit has a pad contact alarm (for example, an Ultima 5 will flash “CH1”/“CH2”), that can indicate a connection or pad-contact problem.

For accessories to purchase with a TENS unit, the most useful add-ons depend on how often you use it, but the most common are:
• Extra electrode pads (for when the originals wear out)
• Different pad sizes (small pads for targeted areas, larger pads for back/hips, etc.)
• Replacement lead wires
• Carrying case
• Rechargeable batteries or charging cable (if compatible with your unit)
• Electrode storage bag or container to keep pads clean and extend their life.

TEST-00079MULTI_INTENTHow should I approach electrode placement Also, what pain conditions does TENS help with?FAILED421120 ms

Expected

{
  "answer": [
    "Proper electrode placement can make a big difference in how comfortable and effective your TENS treatment feels. The goal is to place pads where they can best influence the area of discomfort. Helpful Guidelines: Place pads around the painful area: Position electrodes on either side of the pain or along the surrounding muscles. Avoid placing pads directly over irritated or damaged skin. Keep pads evenly spaced: Proper spacing helps create a consistent stimulation pathway. Use both channels when available: This allows you to treat larger areas or multiple areas at the same time. Adjust placement if needed: Small changes in the pad location can improve comfort and sensation. Follow your device's instructions: Some conditions may have recommended placement patterns.",
    "TENS can help with multiple conditions such as Sciatica, Plantar Fasciitis, Arthritis, Knee Pain, Shoulder Pain, Neck Pain, Tennis Elbow, Carpal Tunnel, Fibromyalgia, Neuropathy, Muscle Spasms, Menstrual Cramps, and Post-Surgical Pain."

    "input\\qa\\Key - VA TENS Unit Bot.csv"
  ]
}

Actual Answer

In many cases, yes, TENS can be used with metal implants, such as joint replacements or surgical hardware. The electrical stimulation from a TENS unit typically does not affect non-electronic metal implants. However, you should check with your healthcare provider before use, especially if the implant is near the treatment area or if you have any type of electronic implant (such as a pacemaker, defibrillator, or nerve stimulator).

Your Quick Start Guide recommends a simple starting point: set the frequency to 150Hz, the pulse width to 250us, and the mode to Constant. Turn the intensity up to a level that feels strong but comfortable, and use the unit for 30-45 minutes, or as long as needed. For your wave form, start with the Asymmetrical Bi-Phasic Rectangular setting, it's the recommended starting point for initial treatment. From there, feel free to adjust based on comfort and results.

Actual Answer

In many cases, yes, TENS can be used with metal implants, such as joint replacements or surgical hardware. The electrical stimulation from a TENS unit typically does not affect non-electronic metal implants. However, you should check with your healthcare provider before use, especially if the implant is near the treatment area or if you have any type of electronic implant (such as a pacemaker, defibrillator, or nerve stimulator).

Actual Answer

PRODUCTS
Electrotherapy Devices • Interferential Therapy

IF 4000 (Analogue Interferential)

Package Includes: Device, batteries, charger, lead wires, 1 pack of 4 electrodes, carrying case and users manual.

Channels: either one or two channel selection.

Output: 0-16 volts (through 500 ohms).

Wave Form: True Sine waveform.

Low battery: Lower Battery Indication.

Frequency – 4000Hz

PRODUCT CODE: IF4000

THERAPY INFO
WARRANTY

DESCRIPTION
Interferential current therapy (IFC): The IF 4000 Interferential is an analogue interferential stimulation device that is used for symptomatic relief and management of post surgical, post traumatic acute, and chronic pain. The IFC 4000 is able to change its amplitude, wavelength, and stimulation mode.

Amplitude: 0 to 16mA each channel.

Stimulation Mode:
1. Continuous, 1/1 abrupt
2. 6/6 ramped and 6/6 abrupt.
3. 10/10 ramped and 10/10 abrupt.

Package Includes
• 4 Reusable Electrode Pads
• Lead Wires
• Batteries
• User Manual & Electrode Placement Guide
• Carrying Case

SPECIFICATIONS
Product Weight (lbs): 1.49375
Length (cm): 25
Width (cm): 20
Height (cm): 8
Weight Code: G

Actual Answer

Soft-Touch electrodes are PMT's premium reusable line: a non-porous tricot backing, conductive wire-mount layer, high-quality carbon layer, and extra-thick gel, with a snug pigtail connector. Oval corners minimize edge curl, and every pad uses latex-free USA gel in a resealable bag to stay fresh. They work well across TENS, EMS, interferential, microcurrent, and galvanic devices.

Most TENS units work with standard snap-style or 2 mm pin-style electrode pads. Compatibility depends on your device's connector type and the pad’s connector (pigtail/pin). PMT Soft-Touch electrodes are universal for almost all TENS and EMS, IF, and Russian units with the pin-type connector.

General guidance PMT provides about TENS electrodes:
- Most TENS electrodes follow a standard 2 mm pin or snap connector size.
- Check your unit’s connector style and recommended pad size before purchasing.
- Use pads labeled specifically for TENS or electrotherapy.
- Coverage: smaller pads are best for targeted areas (wrists, ankles, elbows, small joints); larger pads for larger areas (lower back, shoulders, hips, thighs).
- Flexible pads help on moving joints like knees or shoulders.
- Electrodes typically last about 10–30 uses depending on quality, skin condition, and care.
- Lifespan and effectiveness can be enhanced by rubbing a small drop of water on the pad after use and always storing them on the backing sheet in an airtight, resealable pouch.
- Replace electrodes when the conductive gel starts to wear down, they lose stickiness, or stimulation becomes uneven.

Representative PMT Soft-Touch single-use/reusable conductive electrode product details include:

• Soft-Touch Medical Grade Electrodes – Cloth, 2.0" x 2.0" (PRODUCT CODE: FA2020)
  - Self-adhesive, high quality gel electrodes
  - 45 ohms of resistance
  - Reusable (typically 10–15 or more uses per pad)
  - Universal for all TENS and EMS units with the pin-type connector
  - Available sizes: 2.0" x 2.0", 2.0" Round, 3" Round, Butterfly 6" x 3.3" (various pack configurations)
  - Oval corners help minimize edge curl
  - Average use: 10–15 uses, resealable pouch

• Soft-Touch Medical Grade Electrodes – Cloth, 3.0" Round (PRODUCT CODE: FA3000 2)
  - Same core features: self-adhesive, high quality gel, 45 ohms resistance, 10–15+ uses, universal pin-type connector

• Soft-Touch Carbon Electrodes – Cloth back, PMT gel (PRODUCT CODES: FA2000, FA3000, FA3360 series)
  - Self-adhesive, high quality gel electrodes
  - 45 ohms of resistance
  - Reusable (10–15 or more uses per pad)
  - Available with carbon or blue cloth backing
  - Multiple shapes/sizes: 2.0" x 2.0", 2.0" Round, 3" Round, Butterfly 6" x 3.3", and dual-wired options
  - One dual-wired electrode per package (for certain codes) or 4 electrodes per package (for others)
  - Universal for TENS, EMS, IF, Russian units with pin-type connector
  - Resilient blue fabric back on some models
  - Average use 10–15 uses, resealable pouch

If you tell me which exact “single conductive electrode” model or size you’re interested in (for example, 2" x 2", 3" round, or butterfly), I can narrow this to that specific product code and packaging details.

Actual Answer

Ultima Neuro Foot System

The Ultima Neuro Foot System is an electrotherapy device from Pain Management Technologies designed specifically to help relieve pain associated with peripheral neuropathy in the hands and feet.

Key purpose and use:
- Provides a non-invasive and non-narcotic treatment option for neuropathic pain.
- Targets pain, discomfort, and symptoms associated with early onset peripheral neuropathy.
- Neuropathic pain is often worse at night and can seriously disrupt sleep; the Ultima Neuro is intended as a drug-free, non-surgical option to help relieve this type of pain.

How it works:
- The Ultima Neuro works in tandem with high-quality silver conductive material (such as conductive therapy socks and gloves) to deliver nerve-targeted electrotherapy signals.
- It is based on TENS and micro-current therapy settings but goes beyond a basic TENS unit by narrowing the output parameters to target the relief of peripheral neuropathy in the hands and feet.
- Unlike a basic TENS stimulator, the Ultima Neuro signals are more advanced and precise, with an auto-feedback loop that allows the device to regulate voltage based on different tissue masses (for example, heavier vs. thinner individuals). This auto-adjustability helps tailor treatments to individual tissue impedance.
- The device includes a monophasic waveform that is typically associated with pushing and moving fluids, which is described as particularly effective for pain associated with neuropathy.

Design and components:
- Product: Ultima Neuro Foot System
- Product code: UNEURO-S
- Category: Electrotherapy Devices
- Items sold separately: conductive sock electrode and grounding cuff.
- The system is designed to be used with:
  - Silver conductive therapy socks or gloves (for hands and feet)
  - An electrotherapy cuff used as a dispersive ground pad for the current
- The device is equipped with an ultra-slim, long-life lithium-ion (li-ion) battery for convenience.

Treatment focus:
- Specifically designed to treat pain associated with neuropathy of the hands and feet.
- Intended to help relieve very painful symptoms of early onset neuropathy.
- Uses circumferential electrotherapy stimulation when used with wraps/socks/gloves that cover the extremity.

General wellness context (from PMT information):
- In general, healthy habits such as maintaining optimal weight, avoiding exposure to toxins, following a doctor-recommended exercise program, eating a balanced diet, correcting vitamin deficiencies, and limiting or avoiding alcohol consumption can reduce the physical and emotional effects of peripheral neuropathy.
- Medication is also a common form of treatment for neuropathy but may involve side effects; the Ultima Neuro is described as an additional, non-drug, non-surgical option for symptom relief.

Configuration options mentioned:
- Device only
- Device + sock + cuff + spray
- Device + glove + cuff + spray

If you’d like, I can next help explain how the Ultima Neuro differs from a standard TENS unit, or go over the role of the socks and cuff in more detail.

Powered Cupping Therapy by VacuCup is a compact, powered cupping therapy massager designed for myofascial release, trigger point release, and to help increase circulation, support cell repair, and improve flexibility.

Here is the key PMT information about this device:

• Product name: Powered Cupping Therapy by VacuCup – Myofascial Release, Trigger Point Release, Increase Circulation, Cell Repair and Increase Flexibility. Cupping Therapy Massager for Neck, Back, Quad, Calf and More.

• Technology / Function:
  - POWERED CUPPING TECHNOLOGY: Uses rhythmic alternation of suction and release, which boosts blood circulation faster to the cupping area and supports the recovery process.
  - Red light: The integrated red light is described as driving pain relief, reduction of inflammation, and promoting faster results in healing skin and muscle tissue.

• Modes & Intensity:
  - MULTI MODE: 5 different modes and 5 intensity levels provide varied amounts of suction intensity.
  - The VacuCup Cupper acts like a personal massage therapist and can tailor a massage to individual needs.
  - A simple touch on the control panel allows operation without continuous hand manipulation.

• Therapeutic Use / Effects (general therapy info from PMT):
  - MYOFASCIAL RELEASE: The combination of different treatments enables effective trigger point and myofascial release while enhancing circulation, mobility, and flexibility in minutes.
  - The VacuCup increases blood circulation, which can in turn relieve muscle tension and promote cell repair.
  - Cupping therapy has also been known to help form new connective tissue and create new blood vessels in the tissue.
  - Listed benefits:
    • Helps muscle recovery and soreness
    • Improves blood circulation
    • Promotes cell repair
    • Relieves muscle tension and stress
  - Cupping therapy is described as an ancient treatment modality used to decrease inflammation, stimulate blood flow, and encourage relaxation and well-being.

• Design & Use Areas:
  - COMPACT AND PORTABLE: The compact size makes VacuCup suitable for use on multiple body areas such as the back, shoulder, hips, hamstring, and calves.
  - It is designed to be carried easily, so therapy can be done “anywhere and anytime.”

• Warranty & Product Code:
  - ONE YEAR WARRANTY: If there are any defects from workmanship or materials within one year, PMT will replace the device at no cost to you.
  - PRODUCT CODE: VacuCup

• Specifications:
  - Product Weight (lbs): 1
  - Weight Code: 1 Pounds

Actual Answer

Proper electrode placement can make a big difference in how comfortable and effective your TENS treatment feels. The goal is to place pads where they can best influence the area of discomfort. Helpful Guidelines: Place pads around the painful area: Position electrodes on either side of the pain or along the surrounding muscles. Avoid placing pads directly over irritated or damaged skin. Keep pads evenly spaced: Proper spacing helps create a consistent stimulation pathway. Use both channels when available: This allows you to treat larger areas or multiple areas at the same time. Adjust placement if needed: Small changes in the pad location can improve comfort and sensation. Follow your device's instructions: Some conditions may have recommended placement patterns.

PREMIUM FLEECE – Premium fleece that is super soft and cozy. It is thinner and more nimble than the faux fur, making it perfect for summer use where the AC is blasting, but still warm enough for extra warmth on cold winter mornings.

PATENT PENDING – One-of-a-kind chair blanket that attaches to any office chair. You can wrap the flaps together to create easy-in and easy-out convenience.

SHERPA FUR CHAIR BLANKET – Features a super soft Sherpa FUR interior material. Sherpa Fur is a hybrid material that mixes Sherpa and fleece to create a fur-like feel with sherpa-like warmth that heats the body to perfection. So cuddly you won't want to take it off.

NO RISK HEAT – A safer way to stay warm at the office; no need to use dangerous space heaters.

PERFECT SIZE – One size fits most.

MACHINE WASHABLE – Easy to care for: machine wash cold with like colors and air dry.

Category: Heat Therapy
Available fleece and fur color options associated with this style line include Black Fur, Light Grey Fleece, Raspberry Fleece, Black Fleece, and Blue Fleece.

Actual Answer

Premium Electric Lifting Chair Cushion – by Seat Boost. Electric Power Seat for Complete Sit-to-Stand Lift Supports up to 240 lbs, Black

STAND UP WITH EASE: Fully electric sit to stand lifting mechanism for adults or seniors provides 100% assistance for up to 240 lbs to ensure a gentle and safe lift without having to push forward. It's a great alternative to electric lifting recliners.

DESIGNED FOR MOST ARMCHAIRS, COUCHES, AND SOFAS: The Seat Boost lifts elderly men or women with advanced technology and a powerful yet gentil sounding motor.

COMFORTABLE CUSHION: A memory foam cushion offers the ultimate comfort to allow for sitting for long periods of time. Perfect for seniors, handicap devices or disability aids, or for just those with low body strength. Cord length: 9 feet

SIMPLE CONTROLS: Push button flexible hand control allows the user to adjust the position and height of the seat lift cushion for comfort and flexibility when sitting down and standing up. Stop at any height.

SIMPLE TO CLEAN: The Seat Boost lifting cushion includes a water resistant cushion cover that is easy to clean with a memory foam cushion that's easy to care for.

Power: Electric (120 VAC 60 HZ 0.6 A).
Power Cord Length: 66 inches (5' 6").

UPC: 790566639515
PRODUCT CODE: SBE

Stay Mobile and active

Lifting Seats keep you active and independent. Powered versions provide the right level of assistance based on your particular needs. Those who are able to safely walk on their own can benefit from a lifting chair, with or without a walker, and once standing are active at home or in the community.

How Are Lifting Seats Different?

Lifting chairs help you get up. With that extra umph. These portable sit-to-stand assistive devices push you forward and help you get up from a better position.

Lifting Seats are best suited for those with basic difficulty standing up on their own from a seated position, often due to arthritis, neuromuscular disorders, diabetic disorders, cardiovascular or orthopedic conditions.

Portable Electric Seat Boost
-Gentle lift for those up to 240 lbs.
-Can be used on most armchairs or sofas.
-Easy-to-operate and can be stopped at any height.

SPECIFICATIONS
Product Weight (lbs): 14.5625
Length (cm): 46
Width (cm): 51
Height (cm): 13

Pain Management Technologies (PMT) offers a variety of therapeutic creams and gels that are designed to complement pain management and wellness routines. Here are examples of the types of products and what they are formulated to do based on the information provided:

1. Relaxing Leg Cream by PMT – Nerve Calming, Magnesium & Melatonin, 2.83 oz
• Relief for legs: A powerful leg cream that penetrates deep to relieve discomfort associated with the legs, which can make it harder to get a good night’s rest.
• Nerve-calming & sleep support: The natural formula helps calm the nerves that cause an uneasy sensation, supporting more undisturbed sleep so you can wake up with more energy.
• All-natural formula: Contains all natural ingredients that provide relief and moisturize the legs, with magnesium as the active ingredient.
• Added melatonin: In addition to soothing the body, melatonin helps improve overall sleep and creates a cooling sensation on targeted areas.
• Safe and effective: Targets nerves and absorbs through the skin to provide quick and potent relief before sleep using a safe, natural formula intended for consistent use without unwanted side effects or increased tolerance.
• Nighttime use: Intended for application as part of a nightly routine before bed.

2. Plantar Fasciitis and Heel Spur Cream by PMT – 2.82 oz
• Targeted relief: Therapeutic relief for the foot, plantar fasciitis, and heel spurs; scientifically developed to treat the ligament, tendons, and muscles.
• Fast action: Works within minutes of application.
• Mechanism: The proprietary formula can help increase blood circulation, reduce pain, reduce inflammation, and support treatment of ligaments and muscles affected by plantar fasciitis or heel spurs.
• High concentration of Arnica: Contains a high concentration of Arnica, a powerful and potent clinically-proven ingredient used to help reduce inflammation and treat pain syndromes.
• Superior absorption: Absorbs quickly and can be used to provide targeted relief to other body parts such as back, neck, knee, hip, shoulders, elbows, joints, and muscles.
• Supports natural healing: Helps support the body’s natural healing processes.
• Doctor formulated: Expertly formulated to relieve pain and support treatment of muscles and ligaments.
• Satisfaction guarantee: If you’re not 100% satisfied, PMT invites you to contact them for a refund.

3. Sleep Cream by PMT – Natural Sleeping Aid, 2.83 oz
• Safe & effective: A cream that is safe and effective to use anywhere on the body.
• Application areas: Applying to neck and face, along with chest or arms, allows you to smell the relaxing aroma of lavender while menthol provides a cool, soothing sensation as you fall asleep.
• Travel friendly: The size is suitable for travel to help support a great night’s sleep wherever you are staying.
• Lavender & citrus fruit extract: The relaxing scent of lavender has been shown to naturally calm the body and lower blood pressure, which can subsequently lead to increased overall sleep quality.
• Aromatherapy benefit: Lavender is commonly used in aromatherapies for its calming benefits and can help improve day‑to‑day circadian rhythm.
• Relaxing sleep cream: An all‑natural, calming formula that relaxes the body without directly inducing sleep.
• Essential oil blend: Uses a blend of essential oils to provide a soothing and relaxing sensation that can ease a busy mind and make falling asleep feel more natural.
• All‑natural focus: Formulated with natural ingredients for a gentle, aromatic sleep-support product.

4. Other Therapeutic Creams & Gels Categories Mentioned
Within the broader PMT “Therapeutic Creams and Gels” category, additional products and types are referenced, such as:
• 4% Lidocaine / 3% Menthol Roll-On Pain Relief by Icy Relief (OTC strength; non-greasy formula with Vitamin E, Aloe Vera, and Lavender Oil – 2.5 oz / 73 mL).
• Magnesium Roll-On 3 oz (Zechstein Magnesium + Arginine), for muscle & joint relief for back, neck, legs, and feet, and to support sleep cycle; fast-absorbing, paraben‑free.
• Nerve Target Roll-On Gel (targeted therapeutic treatment for damaged nerves, intractable back pain, joint aches, and muscle spasms).
• Nerve & Neuropathy Cream (topical microvascular & sensory support formula).
• Pain Grenade Roll-On (a roll‑on style topical pain‑relief solution).
• Stress Relief Cream (described as providing relief for stress, easing mind and body, deeply penetrating to alleviate tension and promote a peaceful night’s sleep).
• Nail Repair Pen by Fungi‑Pen (toenail and fingernail repair tool for nail bed damage and discoloration).
• Melaleuca Gel (100% tea tree oil toilet gel stamps) and Melaleuca Spray (100% natural tea tree toilet spray) – odor‑control and hygiene‑focused items also listed within the broader gels category.

Overall, PMT’s therapeutic creams and gels line includes:
• Topical products for localized leg discomfort and nerve‑related sensations.
• Creams specifically targeted to plantar fasciitis and heel spur discomfort.
• Sleep‑support creams using natural aromatherapeutic ingredients like lavender, menthol, and citrus extracts.
• Roll‑on formulas with ingredients such as lidocaine, menthol, magnesium, and Arnica for pain relief, nerve support, and muscle/joint comfort.

Actual Answer

(1pk) Effervescent Tablets

Helps rehydrate feet after soaking
Softens rough heels and callused skin
Supports circulation and foot comfort
Refreshes and deodorizes the feet
Helps maintain skin moisture balance

PRODUCT CODE: Etablets

THERAPY INFO
WARRANTY

DESCRIPTION
Enhance your NerveSpa foot bath with revitalizing effervescent tablets formulated specifically for neuropathic foot care.

After an Epsom salt and warm water soak — which can sometimes leave the skin feeling dry — these tablets help restore moisture balance while delivering skin-supporting nutrients directly into the water.

Hydrolyzed collagen, vitamin C, aloe, and essential supportive ingredients dissolve into the bath to help rehydrate and soften the skin while refreshing tired feet.

How to Use
Usage: Put one tablet into a basin with approximately 4L of warm water. Wait until the tablet is fully dissolved. Soak feet for around 30 minutes and relax. Use 2–3 times per week or as needed.

Ingredient Highlights: Hydrolyzed Collagen, Vitamin C, Zinc Gluconate, Glucose, Citric Acid, Sodium Bicarbonate, Magnesium Stearate, Polyvinyl Pyrrolidone, Mint and Aloe.

SPECIFICATIONS
Product Weight (lbs): 0.125

Actual Answer

Economy Edition Office Desk Chair Wrap by SnuggleBack is a one-of-a-kind chair blanket that attaches to any office chair to keep you warm hands‑free.

Key points:
- Attaches to virtually any office or home office chair using straps on the chair back.
- Flaps wrap together to create an easy-in, easy-out “swaddle” of warmth while you work.
- Designed to end office temperature wars—keeps you warm in winter and when the AC is blasting in summer.
- Uses premium fleece that is super soft, cozy, thinner, and more nimble than faux fur—great for summer use but still warm enough for cold mornings.
- Sherpa fur interior (sherpa + fleece hybrid) gives a fur-like feel with sherpa-like warmth and a very cuddly feel.
- Promoted as an eco‑friendly, safer alternative to space heaters with “no risk heat” (no heater or power source described—just warming by insulation and wrap design).
- One size fits most.
- Machine washable: wash cold with like colors and air dry.
- Product weight: approximately 1 lb.
- Available in Black faux fur with sherpa; other SnuggleBack chair wraps are also listed in grey and blue fleece variants in the same product family.
- Product code noted: Blackfau.

Basic use:
1) Attach the SnuggleBack to the chair back by securing the straps.
2) Drape the blanket over the armrests.
3) Wrap and unwrap around yourself as desired for warmth.
